All Aoperators Aare Acharacterized Aby Ahow Amany Aoperands Athey Acan Aoperate Aon Aat Aa Atime Aand Athe Atypes Aof Aoperations Athey Acan Aexecute. AThey Aare Adivided Ainto Athree Atypes: A
